Q872TE                    CS
* NOTE:  IF NO ZEROS ARE NEEDED IN THE CHILDREN "POPUP", THEN
* WILL HAVE TO CHECK FOR ZEROS AND DO APPROPRIATE FILLS OTHERWISE.
*
* THIS STRANGE METHOD BELOW WILL HELP ELIMINATE AN EXTRA "0" IN THE
* DATA WHEN ONE OR OTHER OF THE TWO QUESTIONS IS SKIPPED/NULL -- IT
* WILL ASSUME THE OTHER QUESTION WILL HAVE DATA.  THIS WILL ULTIMATELY
* FAIL WHEN BOTH QUESTIONS HAVE 0S IN THEM.
* FOR EXAMPLE : J573  574                 569(0)=:/^0=572:
